<script>
const API = window.location.origin;

function escapeHtml(str) {
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.textContent = str;
  return d.innerHTML;
}
let currentTab = 'manual';
let currentJurisdiction = 'AU';
let currentFormat = 'pdf';

// ── Theme toggle ─────────────────────────────────
function toggleTheme() {
  const current = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme');
  const next = current === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', next);
  localStorage.setItem('theme', next);
  document.getElementById('theme-toggle').textContent = next === 'dark' ? '\u2600\uFE0F' : '\uD83C\uDF19';
}
(function setThemeIcon() {
  const t = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme');
  document.getElementById('theme-toggle').textContent = t === 'dark' ? '\u2600\uFE0F' : '\uD83C\uDF19';
})();

// ── Auth ───────────────────────────────────────────
(async function checkAuth() {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
  if (!token) { window.location.href = '/'; return; }
  try {
    let res = await fetch(API + '/auth/me', { headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } });
    if (res.status === 401) {
      const refreshed = await refreshTokenIfNeeded();
      if (refreshed) {
        res = await fetch(API + '/auth/me', { headers: getAuthHeaders() });
      }
    }
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('unauthorized');
    const data = await res.json();
    document.getElementById('user-email').textContent = data.email || '';
    // Auto-fill name and email from authenticated user
    const nameField = document.getElementById('your-name');
    if (nameField && !nameField.value && data.full_name) nameField.value = data.full_name;
    // Health check — disable PDF buttons if not available
    try {
      const hRes = await fetch(API + '/health', { headers: getAuthHeaders() });
      if (hRes.ok) {
        const hData = await hRes.json();
        if (!hData.pdf_available) {
          ['fmt-pdf','fmt-both','ifmt-pdf','ifmt-both'].forEach(id => {
            const el = document.getElementById(id);
            if (el) { el.disabled = true; el.title = 'PDF not available on this server'; }
          });
          if (currentFormat !== 'docx') {
            currentFormat = 'docx';
            document.querySelectorAll('#fmt-docx,#fmt-pdf,#fmt-both').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
            const docxBtn = document.getElementById('fmt-docx');
            if (docxBtn) docxBtn.classList.add('active');
          }
          const notice = document.getElementById('pdf-unavailable-notice');
          if (notice) notice.style.display = 'block';
        }
      }
    } catch (e) { console.warn('Health check failed:', e); }
  } catch (err) {
    localStorage.removeItem('access_token');
    localStorage.removeItem('refresh_token');
    window.location.href = '/';
  }
})();

function getAuthHeaders() {
  return { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + (localStorage.getItem('access_token') || '') };
}

function getToken() {
  return localStorage.getItem('access_token') || '';
}

async function refreshTokenIfNeeded() {
  const rt = localStorage.getItem('refresh_token');
  if (!rt) return false;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(API + '/auth/refresh', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ refresh_token: rt })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return false;
    const data = await res.json();
    localStorage.setItem('access_token', data.access_token);
    localStorage.setItem('refresh_token', data.refresh_token);
    return true;
  } catch { return false; }
}

async function apiCall(url, options = {}) {
  options.headers = { ...(options.headers || {}), ...getAuthHeaders() };
  let res = await fetch(url, options);
  if (res.status === 401) {
    const refreshed = await refreshTokenIfNeeded();
    if (refreshed) {
      options.headers = { ...(options.headers || {}), ...getAuthHeaders() };
      res = await fetch(url, options);
    }
    if (res.status === 401) {
      localStorage.removeItem('access_token');
      localStorage.removeItem('refresh_token');
      window.location.href = '/';
      throw new Error('Session expired');
    }
  }
  return res;
}

function logout() {
  localStorage.removeItem('access_token');
  localStorage.removeItem('refresh_token');
  localStorage.removeItem('user_email');
  window.location.href = '/';
}

// ── Date default ──────────────────────────────────
document.getElementById('swms-date').valueAsDate = new Date();

// ── Review frequency ──────────────────────────────
const REVIEW_OPTIONS = [
  "Before any change is made to the way the construction work is carried out",
  "Before a new system of work is introduced",
  "Before the place where the work is being carried out is changed",
  "If a new hazard is identified",
  "If new information about a hazard becomes available",
  "If a notifiable incident occurs in relation to construction work",
  "If a control measure does not control the risk",
  "A request for a review is received from a health and safety representative",
  "When legislation or codes of practice change, new work methods, products, or equipment are introduced",
  "Minimum 12-monthly review"
];

const REVIEW_DEFAULT_INDICES = [6, 8, 9];

(function buildReviewChecks() {
  const container = document.getElementById('review-checks');
  REVIEW_OPTIONS.forEach((opt, i) => {
    const label = document.createElement('label');
    label.className = 'review-item';
    label.innerHTML = '<input type="checkbox" id="review-' + i + '"> <span>' + escapeHtml(opt) + '</span>';
    container.appendChild(label);
  });
})();

function getReviewText() {
  const checked = REVIEW_OPTIONS.filter((_, i) =>
    document.getElementById('review-' + i).checked
  );
  const selected = checked.length > 0 ? checked : REVIEW_OPTIONS.filter((_, i) => REVIEW_DEFAULT_INDICES.includes(i));
  return "This SWMS will be reviewed: " + selected.join(" \u2014 ");
}

// ── Tab selector ──────────────────────────────────
function selectTab(tab) {
  currentTab = tab;
  document.querySelectorAll('.mode-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById('tab-' + tab).classList.add('active');

  const uploadSection = document.getElementById('upload-section');
  const uploadLabel = document.getElementById('upload-label');
  const formContainer = document.getElementById('form-container');
  const intakePanel = document.getElementById('intake-panel');

  const isIntake = tab === 'intake';
  formContainer.style.display = isIntake ? 'none' : 'block';
  intakePanel.style.display = isIntake ? 'block' : 'none';

  document.querySelectorAll('.selector-row').forEach(el => {
    el.style.display = isIntake ? 'none' : 'flex';
  });
  const genBtn = document.getElementById('btn-generate');
  if (genBtn) genBtn.parentElement.style.display = isIntake ? 'none' : 'block';

  if (tab === 'manual') {
    uploadSection.classList.remove('visible');
  } else if (tab === 'intake') {
    uploadSection.classList.remove('visible');
  } else {
    uploadSection.classList.add('visible');
    uploadLabel.textContent = "Upload your existing SWMS \u2014 we'll identify gaps and upgrade it";
  }

  selectedFiles = [];
  renderFileChips();
  document.getElementById('upload-options').classList.remove('visible');
  document.getElementById('gaps-panel').classList.remove('visible');
  document.getElementById('analyse-status').classList.remove('visible');
}

// ── Jurisdiction selector ─────────────────────────
function selectJurisdiction(code) {
  currentJurisdiction = code;
  document.querySelectorAll('.selector-row .sel-btn').forEach(b => {
    if (b.id && b.id.startsWith('jur-')) {
      b.classList.toggle('active', b.id === 'jur-' + code);
    }
  });
}

// ── Format selector ───────────────────────────────
function selectFormat(fmt) {
  currentFormat = fmt;
  document.querySelectorAll('.sel-btn').forEach(b => {
    if (b.id && b.id.startsWith('fmt-')) {
      b.classList.toggle('active', b.id === 'fmt-' + fmt);
    }
  });
}

// ── Upload handling ───────────────────────────────
let selectedFiles = [];

function toggleUploadOptions() {
  document.getElementById('upload-options').classList.toggle('visible');
}

function openFileUpload() {
  const input = document.createElement('input');
  input.type = 'file';
  input.multiple = true;
  input.accept = '.pdf,.docx,.doc,.txt';
  input.onchange = (e) => handleFilesSelected(e.target.files);
  input.click();
  document.getElementById('upload-options').classList.remove('visible');
}

function openPhotoUpload() {
  const input = document.createElement('input');
  input.type = 'file';
  input.multiple = true;
  input.accept = 'image/*';
  input.setAttribute('capture', 'environment');
  input.onchange = (e) => handleFilesSelected(e.target.files);
  input.click();
  document.getElementById('upload-options').classList.remove('visible');
}

function handleFilesSelected(fileList) {
  selectedFiles = [...selectedFiles, ...Array.from(fileList)];
  renderFileChips();
}

function renderFileChips() {
  const container = document.getElementById('file-chips');
  container.innerHTML = '';
  selectedFiles.forEach((file, i) => {
    const chip = document.createElement('span');
    chip.className = 'file-chip';
    const isImage = file.type.startsWith('image/');
    const icon = isImage ? '&#128247;' : '&#128196;';
    chip.innerHTML = icon + ' ' + escapeHtml(file.name) + ' <button onclick="removeFile(' + i + ')">&times;</button>';
    container.appendChild(chip);
  });
  document.getElementById('btn-analyse').style.display = selectedFiles.length > 0 ? 'block' : 'none';
}

function removeFile(index) {
  selectedFiles.splice(index, 1);
  renderFileChips();
}

let isAnalysing = false;
async function runAnalysis() {
  if (!selectedFiles.length || isAnalysing) return;
  isAnalysing = true;

  const analyseBtn = document.getElementById('btn-analyse');
  analyseBtn.disabled = true;

  const mode = currentTab === 'upgrade' ? '02' : '03';
  const endpoint = mode === '02' ? '/upload/analyse-swms' : '/upload/extract-scope';
  const formData = new FormData();
  selectedFiles.forEach(f => formData.append('files', f));

  const statusEl = document.getElementById('analyse-status');
  statusEl.textContent = 'Analysing document...';
  statusEl.classList.add('visible');

  try {
    const res = await fetch(API + endpoint, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + getToken() },
      body: formData
    });
    if (!res.ok) {
      const err = await res.json();
      throw new Error(err.detail || 'Analysis failed');
    }
    const result = await res.json();
    _scopeContext = result.scope_context || null;
    prefillDirectFields(result.job_data);

    if (mode === '02' && result.gaps && result.gaps.length) {
      showGaps(result.gaps);
      statusEl.textContent = 'Analysis complete \u2014 ' + result.gaps.length + ' gaps identified. Review fields and generate.';
    } else {
      statusEl.textContent = 'Scope extracted. Review fields and generate.';
    }
  } catch (err) {
    statusEl.textContent = 'Error: ' + err.message;
  } finally {
    isAnalysing = false;
    analyseBtn.disabled = false;
  }
}

function prefillDirectFields(jobData) {
  if (jobData.pcbu_name) setIfEmpty('pcbu-name', jobData.pcbu_name);
  if (jobData.manager_name) setIfEmpty('your-name', jobData.manager_name);
  if (jobData.project_address) setIfEmpty('site-address', jobData.project_address);
  if (jobData.description) setIfEmpty('job-description', jobData.description);
  if (jobData.principal_contractor) setIfEmpty('principal-contractor', jobData.principal_contractor);
}

function setIfEmpty(id, value) {
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (el && !el.value.trim()) el.value = value;
}

// ── Gaps panel ────────────────────────────────────
function showGaps(gaps) {
  const panel = document.getElementById('gaps-panel');
  const list = document.getElementById('gaps-list');
  const count = document.getElementById('gaps-count');
  panel.classList.add('visible');
  count.textContent = gaps.length + ' gap' + (gaps.length !== 1 ? 's' : '') + ' identified';
  list.innerHTML = gaps.map(g => '<li>' + escapeHtml(g) + '</li>').join('');
}

function toggleGaps() {
  const body = document.getElementById('gaps-body');
  const icon = document.getElementById('gaps-icon');
  const open = body.classList.toggle('open');
  icon.textContent = open ? '\u2212' : '+';
}

// ── Build payload ─────────────────────────────────
function getField(id) {
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  return el ? el.value.trim() : '';
}

function getSelectedFormat() {
  return currentFormat;
}

function buildPayload() {
  const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-AU', {
    day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric'
  });

  const description = getField('job-description');
  const siteAddress = getField('site-address');
  const pcbu = getField('pcbu-name');
  const pc = getField('principal-contractor') || pcbu;
  const manager = getField('your-name');
  const supervisor = getField('compliance-person');
  const reviewer = getField('review-person');
  const swmsDate = getField('swms-date') || today;
  const reviewText = getReviewText();

  return {
    description: description,
    project_meta: {
      project_name: siteAddress || 'Untitled Project',
      site_address: siteAddress,
      project_address: siteAddress,
      pcbu_name: pcbu,
      principal_contractor: pc,
      manager_name: manager,
      manager: manager,
      supervisor: supervisor,
      reviewer: reviewer,
      work_activity: description,
      date: swmsDate,
      review_frequency: reviewText,
      version: '1.0'
    },
    jurisdiction: currentJurisdiction,
    format: getSelectedFormat()
  };
}

// ── Status helpers ────────────────────────────────
function setStep(id, state) {
  document.getElementById(id).className = 'status-step ' + state;
}

function showStatusPanel() {
  document.getElementById('form-container').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('btn-generate').parentElement.style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('upload-section').classList.remove('visible');
  document.getElementById('gaps-panel').classList.remove('visible');
  document.querySelector('.mode-tabs').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('status-panel').style.display = 'block';
  ['step-analyse','step-infer','step-tasks','step-render','step-pdf','step-download'].forEach(s => setStep(s, 'pending'));
  document.getElementById('step-pdf').style.display = (currentFormat === 'pdf' || currentFormat === 'both') ? 'flex' : 'none';
}

// ── Generate ──────────────────────────────────────
let lastInference = null;
let lastTasks = null;
let lastMeta = null;
let pendingBlob = null;
let pendingFilename = null;
let pendingBothData = null;
let _scopeContext = null;

async function generate() {
  const genBtn = document.getElementById('btn-generate');
  if (genBtn.disabled) return;
  genBtn.disabled = true;

  const payload = buildPayload();
  if (!payload.description) {
    alert('Please enter a job description.');
    genBtn.disabled = false;
    return;
  }

  showStatusPanel();
  lastMeta = payload.project_meta;

  // Show "do not refresh" warning + beforeunload guard
  document.getElementById('gen-warning').style.display = 'block';
  window.onbeforeunload = function() {
    return 'Your SWMS is still generating. If you leave now your document will be lost.';
  };

  lastTasks = [];
  lastInference = {};

  try {
    // Stream generation
    setStep('step-analyse', 'active');
    const streamRes = await fetch(API + '/generate/stream', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + getToken()
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        description: payload.description,
        project_meta: payload.project_meta,
        force_full: true,
        jurisdiction: currentJurisdiction,
        scope_context: _scopeContext
      })
    });
    if (!streamRes.ok) throw new Error('Stream failed: ' + streamRes.status);

    const reader = streamRes.body.getReader();
    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    let buffer = '';

    while (true) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
      const parts = buffer.split('\n\n');
      buffer = parts.pop();
      for (const part of parts) {
        const line = part.replace(/^data:\s*/, '').trim();
        if (!line) continue;
        const event = JSON.parse(line);
        if (event.type === 'route') {
          setStep('step-analyse', 'done');
          setStep('step-infer', 'active');
          showInference(event.inference);
          lastInference = event.inference;
        } else if (event.type === 'task_count') {
          setStep('step-infer', 'done');
          setStep('step-tasks', 'active');
          document.getElementById('step-tasks-text').textContent =
            'Generating ' + event.count + ' tasks\u2026';
        } else if (event.type === 'task') {
          lastTasks.push(event.task);
          document.getElementById('step-tasks-text').textContent =
            'Generating task ' + event.index + ' of ' + event.total + '\u2026';
        } else if (event.type === 'done') {
          setStep('step-tasks', 'done');
        } else if (event.type === 'error') {
          throw new Error(event.message);
        }
      }
    }

    // Step 4: Render
    setStep('step-render', 'active');
    const renderPayload = {
      tasks: lastTasks,
      project_meta: payload.project_meta,
      inference: lastInference,
      jurisdiction: currentJurisdiction
    };
    const renderResult = await renderDocument('/render/docx', '/render/pdf', '/render/both', renderPayload, payload.project_meta);
    setStep('step-render', 'done');

    // Step 4b: PDF step
    if (currentFormat === 'pdf' || currentFormat === 'both') {
      setStep('step-pdf', 'active');
      await sleep(100);
      setStep('step-pdf', 'done');
    }

    // Step 5: Pass to review page
    setStep('step-download', 'active');
    pendingBlob = renderResult.blob;
    pendingFilename = renderResult.filename;
    pendingBothData = renderResult.bothData;
    goToReview();
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(err);
    ['step-analyse','step-infer','step-tasks','step-render','step-pdf','step-download'].forEach(s => setStep(s, 'pending'));
    const statusPanel = document.getElementById('status-panel');
    if (statusPanel) {
      const errDiv = document.createElement('div');
      errDiv.style.cssText = 'color:#dc2626;text-align:center;padding:16px;font-weight:600;';
      errDiv.textContent = 'Error: ' + err.message;
      statusPanel.appendChild(errDiv);
    }
  } finally {
    document.getElementById('gen-warning').style.display = 'none';
    window.onbeforeunload = null;
    document.getElementById('btn-generate').disabled = false;
  }
}

function buildFilename(meta) {
  const site = (meta.site_address || meta.project_name || 'SWMS')
    .replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g, '-').replace(/\s+/g, '-').substring(0, 40);
  const now = new Date();
  const dd = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  const mm = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const yyyy = now.getFullYear();
  return 'SWMS-' + site + '-' + dd + mm + yyyy + '-V01.docx';
}

async function renderDocument(docxUrl, pdfUrl, bothUrl, payload, meta) {
  const fmt = currentFormat;
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 30000);
  const opts = {
    method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(payload), signal: controller.signal
  };
  try {
    if (fmt === 'docx') {
      const res = await apiCall(API + docxUrl, opts);
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Render failed: ' + res.status);
      return { blob: await res.blob(), filename: buildFilename(meta), bothData: null };
    }
    if (fmt === 'pdf') {
      const res = await apiCall(API + pdfUrl, opts);
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('PDF render failed: ' + res.status);
      return { blob: await res.blob(), filename: buildFilename(meta).replace('.docx', '.pdf'), bothData: null };
    }
    // both
    const res = await apiCall(API + bothUrl, opts);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Render both failed: ' + res.status);
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.error) throw new Error(data.error);
    return { blob: null, filename: data.docx_filename, bothData: data };
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.name === 'AbortError') throw new Error('Render is taking longer than expected — please try again');
    throw err;
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
  }
}

function downloadBase64(b64, filename, mimeType) {
  const bytes = atob(b64);
  const arr = new Uint8Array(bytes.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) { arr[i] = bytes.charCodeAt(i); }
  const blob = new Blob([arr], { type: mimeType });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url; a.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url); a.remove();
}

function sleep(ms) { return new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms)); }

function showInference(inf) {
  const panel = document.getElementById('inference-panel');
  const list = document.getElementById('inference-list');

  const items = [
    ['HRCW', inf.hrcw ? 'Yes \u2014 ' + (inf.hrcw_category || '') : 'No'],
    ['Codes Detected', (inf.ccvs_codes || []).join(', ') || '\u2014'],
    ['PPE Identified', (inf.ppe || []).join(', ') || '\u2014'],
    ['Permits Required', (inf.permits || []).join(', ') || '\u2014'],
    ['Plant Detected', (inf.plant || []).join(', ') || '\u2014'],
  ];
  list.innerHTML = items.map(([k, v]) => '<dt>' + escapeHtml(k) + '</dt><dd>' + escapeHtml(v) + '</dd>').join('');
}

function acceptAndDownload() {
  document.getElementById('disclaimer-overlay').classList.remove('visible');
  setStep('step-download', 'done');
  document.getElementById('step-download-text').textContent = 'Disclaimer accepted \u2014 downloaded';

  const panel = document.getElementById('download-panel');
  panel.style.display = 'block';

  if (pendingBothData) {
    const data = pendingBothData;
    document.getElementById('dl-done-label').innerHTML = '&#10003; Your SWMS is ready (Word + PDF)';
    document.getElementById('dl-filename').textContent = data.docx_filename + ' + ' + data.pdf_filename;
    const btn = document.getElementById('dl-btn');
    btn.textContent = 'Download SWMS (Both)';
    btn.onclick = function() {
      downloadBase64(data.docx, data.docx_filename,
        'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document');
      setTimeout(function() {
        downloadBase64(data.pdf, data.pdf_filename, 'application/pdf');
      }, 600);
    };
    btn.click();
  } else {
    document.getElementById('dl-done-label').innerHTML = '&#10003; Your SWMS is ready';
    document.getElementById('dl-filename').textContent = pendingFilename;
    const btn = document.getElementById('dl-btn');
    btn.textContent = 'Download SWMS';
    btn.onclick = function() {
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(pendingBlob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url; a.download = pendingFilename; a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    };
    btn.click();
  }
}

function declineDownload() {
  document.getElementById('disclaimer-overlay').classList.remove('visible');
  pendingBlob = null; pendingFilename = null; pendingBothData = null;
  setStep('step-download', 'done');
  document.getElementById('step-download-text').textContent = 'Disclaimer declined \u2014 document discarded';
  document.getElementById('download-panel').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('declined-msg').style.display = 'block';
}

// ── Jurisdiction mismatch check ───────────────────
let detectedJurisdiction = null;
function checkJurisdiction() {
  const desc = getField('job-description');
  if (!desc || desc.length < 10) {
    document.getElementById('jur-warning').style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }
  fetch(API + '/check-jurisdiction?q=' + encodeURIComponent(desc) + '&selected=' + currentJurisdiction)
    .then(r => r.json())
    .then(data => {
      const warn = document.getElementById('jur-warning');
      if (data.mismatch) {
        detectedJurisdiction = data.detected;
        document.getElementById('jur-warn-text').textContent = data.warning;
        document.getElementById('jur-switch-btn').textContent = 'Switch to ' + data.detected;
        warn.style.display = 'block';
      } else {
        warn.style.display = 'none';
        detectedJurisdiction = null;
      }
    })
    .catch(() => { console.warn('Jurisdiction check failed — continuing with selected jurisdiction'); });
}

function switchDetectedJurisdiction() {
  if (detectedJurisdiction) {
    selectJurisdiction(detectedJurisdiction);
    document.getElementById('jur-warning').style.display = 'none';
    detectedJurisdiction = null;
  }
}

let _intakeFields = {};
let _intakeEdited = new Set();
let _intakeLastUrl = null;
let _intakeLastFilename = null;

(function() {
  const origSelectTab = window.selectTab || selectTab;
  const _origBody = origSelectTab.toString();
  // Patch: show/hide intake panel on tab switch
  const _patchedSelectTab = window.selectTab;
  if (_patchedSelectTab) {
    const _orig = _patchedSelectTab;
    window.selectTab = function(tab) {
      const intakePanel = document.getElementById('intake-panel');
      if (intakePanel) {
        intakePanel.style.display = tab === 'intake' ? 'block' : 'none';
      }
      return _orig(tab);
    };
  }
})();

function intakePickFile() {
  document.getElementById('intake-file-input').click();
}

function intakeFileChosen(input) {
  if (input.files.length) intakeRunExtract(input.files[0]);
}

function intakeDrop(e) {
  e.preventDefault();
  const f = e.dataTransfer.files[0];
  if (f) intakeRunExtract(f);
}

async function intakeRunExtract(file) {
  const status = document.getElementById('intake-upload-status');
  status.textContent = 'Extracting\u2026';
  status.style.color = 'var(--text-muted)';
  document.getElementById('intake-drop').style.opacity = '0.5';

  const fd = new FormData();
  fd.append('files', file);

  try {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
    const res = await fetch('/intake/extract', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` },
      body: fd,
    });
    if (!res.ok) {
      const err = await res.json();
      throw new Error(err.detail || 'Extraction failed');
    }
    const data = await res.json();
    _intakeFields = data.fields || {};
    _intakeEdited.clear();
    intakePopulateForm(data);
    document.getElementById('intake-upload-zone').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('intake-review-zone').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('istep-upload').classList.remove('active');
    document.getElementById('istep-review').classList.add('active');
  } catch (err) {
    status.textContent = 'Error: ' + err.message;
    status.style.color = '#c00';
    document.getElementById('intake-drop').style.opacity = '1';
  }
}

function intakePopulateForm(data) {
  const fields     = data.fields     || {};
  const confidence = data.confidence || {};
  const sources    = data.sources    || {};

  const BADGE_MAP = {
    high:     { text: '\u2713 Found',    cls: 'badge-high'   },
    medium:   { text: '~ Inferred', cls: 'badge-medium' },
    low:      { text: '? Assumed',  cls: 'badge-low'    },
    inferred: { text: '? Assumed',  cls: 'badge-low'    },
    absent:   { text: '! Required', cls: 'badge-absent' },
    missing:  { text: '! Required', cls: 'badge-absent' },
  };

  const FIELD_KEYS = [
    'pcbu_name','project_address','manager_name','principal_contractor',
    'title','work_activity_summary','description',
    'access_method','building_type','storeys','occupancy'
  ];

  for (const key of FIELD_KEYS) {
    const input  = document.getElementById('iinput-' + key);
    const badge  = document.getElementById('ibadge-' + key);
    const source = document.getElementById('isource-' + key);
    if (!input) continue;

    const val  = fields[key] || '';
    const conf = confidence[key] || 'absent';
    const src  = sources[key]    || '';

    if (Array.isArray(val)) {
      input.value = val.join(', ');
    } else {
      input.value = val;
    }

    if (badge) {
      const b = BADGE_MAP[conf] || BADGE_MAP.absent;
      badge.textContent  = b.text;
      badge.className    = 'intake-badge ' + b.cls;
      badge.title        = src || '';
    }
    if (source && src) {
      source.textContent = 'Source: "' + src + '"';
    }
  }

  // Summary bar
  const bar = document.getElementById('intake-summary-bar');
  const absent = data.absent_count || 0;
  const missing = (data.missing_required || []).length;
  bar.innerHTML = absent > 0
    ? `<span class="bar-warn">\u26a0\ufe0f ${absent} field${absent>1?'s':''} not found \u2014 review highlighted fields before generating</span>`
    : `<span class="bar-ok">\u2713 All fields extracted \u2014 review and confirm</span>`;

  // HRCW flags — editable
  let hrcw = fields.hrcw_categories;
  if (!hrcw) hrcw = [];
  else if (typeof hrcw === 'string') hrcw = hrcw.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  else if (!Array.isArray(hrcw)) hrcw = [];
  _intakeFields.hrcw_categories = hrcw.slice();
  const hrcwSec = document.getElementById('intake-hrcw-section');
  hrcwSec.style.display = 'block';
  intakeHrcwRender();

  // Missing warning
  if (missing > 0) {
    const warn = document.getElementById('intake-missing-warning');
    warn.style.display = 'block';
    const labels = {
      pcbu_name: 'Company Name', project_address: 'Site Address',
      description: 'Scope Description', work_activity_summary: 'Work Activity',
      title: 'Document Title'
    };
    warn.innerHTML = '\u26a0\ufe0f Required fields missing: ' +
      (data.missing_required || []).map(f => labels[f] || f).join(', ') +
      '. Please fill these before generating.';
  }

  // Pre-fill date
  const dateEl = document.getElementById('iinput-swms-date');
  if (dateEl) {
    const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-AU', {
      day:'2-digit', month:'2-digit', year:'numeric'
    });
    dateEl.value = today;
  }

  // Pre-fill compliance/review person from manager_name
  const compEl = document.getElementById('iinput-compliance-person');
  const revEl = document.getElementById('iinput-review-person');
  if (compEl && fields.manager_name) compEl.value = fields.manager_name;
  if (revEl && fields.manager_name) revEl.value = fields.manager_name;

  // Populate review options from existing REVIEW_OPTIONS if available
  const reviewDiv = document.getElementById('intake-review-options');
  if (reviewDiv && typeof REVIEW_OPTIONS !== 'undefined') {
    reviewDiv.innerHTML = REVIEW_OPTIONS.map((opt, i) =>
      `<label class="review-item">
        <input type="checkbox" ${REVIEW_DEFAULT_INDICES.includes(i) ? 'checked' : ''}>
        <span>${opt}</span>
       </label>`
    ).join('');
  }

  // Force show compliance/jurisdiction/format sections
  const intakeSections = document.querySelectorAll('#intake-review-zone .intake-section');
  intakeSections.forEach(s => s.style.display = 'block');
}

function intakeHrcwRender() {
  const div = document.getElementById('intake-hrcw-flags');
  const flags = _intakeFields.hrcw_categories || [];
  div.innerHTML = '';
  flags.forEach((h, i) => {
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'hrcw-flag-row';
    const inp = document.createElement('input');
    inp.type = 'text';
    inp.value = h;
    inp.setAttribute('aria-label', 'HRCW flag');
    inp.addEventListener('input', () => {
      _intakeFields.hrcw_categories[i] = inp.value;
    });
    const del = document.createElement('button');
    del.className = 'hrcw-flag-del';
    del.textContent = '\u00d7';
    del.title = 'Remove flag';
    del.addEventListener('click', () => intakeHrcwDelete(i));
    row.appendChild(inp);
    row.appendChild(del);
    div.appendChild(row);
  });
  const add = document.createElement('button');
  add.className = 'hrcw-flag-add';
  add.textContent = '+ Add HRCW flag';
  add.addEventListener('click', intakeHrcwAdd);
  div.appendChild(add);
}

function intakeHrcwDelete(i) {
  if (!_intakeFields.hrcw_categories) return;
  _intakeFields.hrcw_categories.splice(i, 1);
  intakeHrcwRender();
}

function intakeHrcwAdd() {
  if (!_intakeFields.hrcw_categories) _intakeFields.hrcw_categories = [];
  _intakeFields.hrcw_categories.push('');
  intakeHrcwRender();
  // Focus the new input
  const rows = document.querySelectorAll('#intake-hrcw-flags .hrcw-flag-row');
  if (rows.length) rows[rows.length - 1].querySelector('input').focus();
}

function intakeDownloadResult() {
  if (!_intakeLastUrl) {
    alert('No document available. Please generate a SWMS first.');
    return;
  }
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = _intakeLastUrl;
  a.download = _intakeLastFilename || 'SWMS.docx';
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
}

function intakeMarkEdited(key) {
  _intakeEdited.add(key);
  const badge = document.getElementById('ibadge-' + key);
  if (badge && !badge.classList.contains('badge-edited')) {
    badge.textContent = '\u270f Edited';
    badge.className   = 'intake-badge badge-edited';
  }
}

async function intakeConfirmGenerate() {
  // Collect current field values from form
  const FIELD_KEYS = [
    'pcbu_name','project_address','manager_name','principal_contractor',
    'title','work_activity_summary','description',
    'access_method','building_type','storeys','occupancy'
  ];
  const fields = {};
  for (const key of FIELD_KEYS) {
    const el = document.getElementById('iinput-' + key);
    if (el) fields[key] = el.value.trim();
  }
  // Include edited HRCW flags (filter out blank entries)
  fields.hrcw_categories = (_intakeFields.hrcw_categories || []).filter(h => h.trim());

  if (!fields.description) {
    alert('Scope Description is required before generating.');
    document.getElementById('iinput-description').focus();
    return;
  }

  // Build project_meta and scope_context for /generate/stream
  const intakeMeta = {
    pcbu_name:             fields.pcbu_name || '',
    project_address:       fields.project_address || '',
    manager_name:          fields.manager_name || '',
    principal_contractor:  fields.principal_contractor || '',
    title:                 fields.title || '',
    work_activity_summary: fields.work_activity_summary || '',
    supervisor:            fields.manager_name || '',
    work_activity:         fields.work_activity_summary || '',
    description:           fields.description,
  };
  let intakeScope = null;
  if (fields.access_method || fields.building_type || fields.storeys || fields.occupancy) {
    intakeScope = {
      access_method:   fields.access_method || '',
      building_type:   fields.building_type || '',
      storeys:         fields.storeys || '',
      occupancy:       fields.occupancy || '',
      hrcw_categories: fields.hrcw_categories || [],
    };
  }

  document.getElementById('intake-review-zone').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('intake-generating-zone').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('istep-review').classList.remove('active');
  document.getElementById('istep-generate').classList.add('active');

  const statusPanel = document.getElementById('intake-status-panel');
  statusPanel.innerHTML = '<div class="status-step">\u23f3 Analysing job description\u2026</div>';

  let intakeTasks = [];
  let intakeInference = {};

  try {
    // Use SSE streaming — same as main generate flow
    const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
    const streamRes = await fetch(API + '/generate/stream', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token,
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        description: fields.description,
        project_meta: intakeMeta,
        force_full: true,
        jurisdiction: _intakeJur,
        scope_context: intakeScope,
      }),
    });
    if (!streamRes.ok) {
      let msg = 'Generation failed';
      try { const err = await streamRes.json(); msg = err.detail || msg; } catch {}
      throw new Error(msg);
    }

    const reader = streamRes.body.getReader();
    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    let buffer = '';

    while (true) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
      const parts = buffer.split('\n\n');
      buffer = parts.pop();
      for (const part of parts) {
        const line = part.replace(/^data:\s*/, '').trim();
        if (!line) continue;
        let event;
        try { event = JSON.parse(line); } catch { continue; }
        intakeHandleStreamEvent(event, statusPanel);
        if (event.type === 'route') {
          intakeInference = event.inference || {};
        } else if (event.type === 'task') {
          intakeTasks.push(event.task);
        } else if (event.type === 'error') {
          throw new Error(event.message);
        }
      }
    }

    // Render step
    statusPanel.innerHTML += '<div class="status-step">\u23f3 Rendering document\u2026</div>';
    const renderPayload = {
      tasks: intakeTasks,
      project_meta: intakeMeta,
      inference: intakeInference,
      jurisdiction: _intakeJur,
    };
    const renderUrl = _intakeFmt === 'pdf' ? '/render/pdf' : '/render/docx';
    const renderRes = await fetch(API + renderUrl, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token },
      body: JSON.stringify(renderPayload),
    });
    if (!renderRes.ok) throw new Error('Render failed: ' + renderRes.status);

    const blob = await renderRes.blob();
    if (blob.size < 100) throw new Error('Document generation failed \u2014 empty response');

    // Build filename and trigger download
    const addr = (fields.project_address || 'SWMS').replace(/,/g, '').trim();
    const safe = addr.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9 -]/g, '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, '-').substring(0, 40);
    const now = new Date();
    const dd = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    const mm = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const ext = _intakeFmt === 'pdf' ? 'pdf' : 'docx';
    const fname = `SWMS-${safe}-${dd}${mm}${now.getFullYear()}-V01.${ext}`;

    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = fname;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    a.remove();
    _intakeLastUrl = url;
    _intakeLastFilename = fname;

    statusPanel.innerHTML +=
      '<div class="status-step" style="color:#2e7d32">\u2713 SWMS generated \u2014 downloading\u2026</div>' +
      '<div style="margin-top:16px;display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;">' +
        '<button class="btn-generate" style="flex:1;min-width:160px" onclick="intakeDownloadResult()">' +
          '\u2B07\uFE0F Download again' +
        '</button>' +
        '<button class="btn-secondary" style="flex:1;min-width:140px" onclick="intakeReset()">' +
          '\u2190 Generate another' +
        '</button>' +
      '</div>';
  } catch (err) {
    statusPanel.innerHTML +=
      `<div class="status-step" style="color:#c00">Error: ${err.message}</div>`;
  }
}

function intakeHandleStreamEvent(event, panel) {
  if (event.type === 'route') {
    panel.innerHTML = '<div class="status-step">\u2713 Analysed \u2014 running inference\u2026</div>';
  } else if (event.type === 'task_count') {
    panel.innerHTML +=
      `<div class="status-step">\u2713 Inference done \u2014 ${event.count} tasks identified</div>`;
  } else if (event.type === 'task') {
    const pct = Math.round(((event.index + 1) / event.total) * 100);
    const bar = document.getElementById('intake-progress-bar');
    if (!bar) {
      panel.innerHTML +=
        `<div class="status-step">
           <div>Writing controls\u2026 ${event.index+1} of ${event.total}</div>
           <div class="progress-bar-wrap">
             <div class="progress-bar" id="intake-progress-bar"
                  style="width:${pct}%"></div>
           </div>
         </div>`;
    } else {
      bar.style.width = pct + '%';
      bar.parentElement.previousElementSibling.textContent =
        `Writing controls\u2026 ${event.index+1} of ${event.total}`;
    }
  } else if (event.type === 'done') {
    panel.innerHTML +=
      '<div class="status-step">\u2713 All tasks complete \u2014 rendering document\u2026</div>';
  } else if (event.type === 'error') {
    panel.innerHTML +=
      `<div class="status-step" style="color:#c00">Error: ${event.message}</div>`;
  }
}

let _intakeJur = 'AU';
let _intakeFmt = 'docx';

function intakeSelectJur(code) {
  _intakeJur = code;
  ['AU','NZ','UK','US','CA'].forEach(c => {
    const btn = document.getElementById('ijur-' + c);
    if (btn) btn.classList.toggle('active', c === code);
  });
}

function intakeSelectFmt(fmt) {
  _intakeFmt = fmt;
  ['docx','pdf','both'].forEach(f => {
    const btn = document.getElementById('ifmt-' + f);
    if (btn) btn.classList.toggle('active', f === fmt);
  });
}

function intakeReset() {
  _intakeFields = {};
  _intakeEdited.clear();
  if (_intakeLastUrl) { URL.revokeObjectURL(_intakeLastUrl); _intakeLastUrl = null; }
  _intakeLastFilename = null;
  document.getElementById('intake-upload-zone').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('intake-review-zone').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('intake-generating-zone').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('intake-upload-status').textContent = '';
  document.getElementById('intake-drop').style.opacity = '1';
  document.getElementById('intake-missing-warning').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('intake-hrcw-section').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('istep-upload').classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('istep-review').classList.remove('active');
  document.getElementById('istep-generate').classList.remove('active');
}

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>

<script>
const API = window.location.origin;

// ── Theme toggle ─────────────────────────────────
function toggleTheme() {
  const current = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme');
  const next = current === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', next);
  localStorage.setItem('theme', next);
  document.getElementById('theme-toggle').textContent = next === 'dark' ? '\u2600\uFE0F' : '\uD83C\uDF19';
}
(function setThemeIcon() {
  const t = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme');
  document.getElementById('theme-toggle').textContent = t === 'dark' ? '\u2600\uFE0F' : '\uD83C\uDF19';
})();

// ── Auth check ────────────────────────────────────
(async function checkAuth() {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
  if (!token) { window.location.href = '/'; return; }
  try {
    let res = await fetch(API + '/auth/me', {
      headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token }
    });
    if (res.status === 401) {
      const refreshed = await refreshToken();
      if (refreshed) {
        res = await fetch(API + '/auth/me', {
          headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + localStorage.getItem('access_token') }
        });
      }
    }
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('unauthorized');
    const data = await res.json();
    document.getElementById('user-email').textContent = data.email || '';
  } catch (err) {
    localStorage.removeItem('access_token');
    localStorage.removeItem('refresh_token');
    window.location.href = '/';
  }
})();

async function refreshToken() {
  const rt = localStorage.getItem('refresh_token');
  if (!rt) return false;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(API + '/auth/refresh', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ refresh_token: rt })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return false;
    const data = await res.json();
    localStorage.setItem('access_token', data.access_token);
    localStorage.setItem('refresh_token', data.refresh_token);
    return true;
  } catch { return false; }
}

function logout() {
  localStorage.removeItem('access_token');
  localStorage.removeItem('refresh_token');
  localStorage.removeItem('user_email');
  window.location.href = '/';
}

// ── Coming Soon toast ─────────────────────────────
function showComingSoon() {
  const toast = document.getElementById('toast');
  toast.classList.add('visible');
  setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove('visible'), 2500);
}
</script>

.footer{background:var(--footer-bg);padding:20px 40px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center}

  <!-- Right column: inference sidebar -->
  <div class="review-sidebar">
    <div class="sidebar-card" id="sidebar-hrcw"></div>
    <div class="sidebar-card" id="sidebar-ppe">
      <div class="sidebar-title">PPE Required</div>
      <div id="ppe-list"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="sidebar-card" id="sidebar-certs">
      <div class="sidebar-title">Certifications</div>
      <div id="certs-list"></div>
    </div>

    I have reviewed this SWMS and confirm it reflects actual site conditions

    <button class="confirm-btn" id="confirm-btn" disabled onclick="confirmAndDownload()">Confirm &amp; Download</button>
    <span class="confirm-error" id="confirm-error"></span>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Disclaimer modal -->
<div class="disclaimer-overlay" id="disclaimer-overlay">
  <div class="disclaimer-box">
    <div class="disclaimer-header">
      <h2>Important Disclaimer</h2>
      <p>Please read before downloading your SWMS</p>
    </div>
    <div class="disclaimer-scroll">
      <h3>AI-Generated Document</h3>
      <p>This Safe Work Method Statement was generated using artificial intelligence. While it has been built against Australian WHS Regulations and industry standards, it is a starting point only.</p>
      <h3>Professional Review Required</h3>
      <p>A competent person with relevant site knowledge must review, verify, and approve this SWMS before it is used on any worksite. The SWMS must reflect actual site conditions, equipment, and personnel.</p>
      <h3>No Liability</h3>
      <p>AuditCo Pty Ltd (trading as Safe Method) accepts no liability for any loss, damage, injury, or death arising from the use of this AI-generated document. The person conducting the business or undertaking (PCBU) retains full responsibility for workplace safety.</p>
      <h3>Site-Specific Verification</h3>
      <p>Before use, verify: all hazards are identified for your specific site; controls are adequate and available; PPE items are on hand; permits and licences are current; emergency procedures are established; workers have been inducted using this SWMS.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="disclaimer-bottom">
      <label class="disclaimer-check">
        <input type="checkbox" id="disclaimer-checkbox" onchange="onDisclaimerCheck()">
        I understand this is an AI-generated document and I accept responsibility for professional review before site use
      </label>
      <div class="disclaimer-actions">
        <button class="btn-decline" onclick="declineDisclaimer()">Decline</button>
        <button class="btn-accept" id="disclaimer-accept-btn" disabled onclick="acceptAndDownload()">Accept &amp; Download</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

<script>
// ── State ────────────────────────────────────────
let tasks = [];
let inference = {};
let meta = {};
let pendingBlob = null;
let pendingFilename = '';

const API = window.location.origin;

// ── Init ─────────────────────────────────────────
(function init() {
  const rawTasks = localStorage.getItem('swms_review_tasks');
  const rawInference = localStorage.getItem('swms_review_inference');
  const rawMeta = localStorage.getItem('swms_review_meta');

  if (!rawTasks || !rawInference || !rawMeta) {
    window.location.href = '/swms';
    return;
  }

  try {
    tasks = JSON.parse(rawTasks);
    inference = JSON.parse(rawInference);
    meta = JSON.parse(rawMeta);
  } catch {
    window.location.href = '/swms';
    return;
  }

  // Page heading
  const title = meta.project_name || meta.title || 'Review SWMS';
  const sub = meta.site_address || meta.project_address || '';
  document.getElementById('page-title').textContent = title;
  document.getElementById('page-sub').textContent = sub;

  // Nav email
  const email = localStorage.getItem('user_email') || meta.manager_name || '';
  document.getElementById('nav-email').textContent = email;

  // Reviewed by
  const reviewer = localStorage.getItem('user_name') || meta.supervisor || meta.manager_name || 'Reviewer';
  document.getElementById('reviewed-by').textContent = 'Reviewed by ' + reviewer;

  renderTaskCards();
  renderSidebar();
  initTheme();
})();

// ── Theme ────────────────────────────────────────
function initTheme() {
  const t = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme');
  document.getElementById('theme-icon').textContent = t === 'dark' ? '\u2600\ufe0f' : '\ud83c\udf19';
}
function toggleTheme() {
  const cur = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme');
  const next = cur === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', next);
  localStorage.setItem('theme', next);
  document.getElementById('theme-icon').textContent = next === 'dark' ? '\u2600\ufe0f' : '\ud83c\udf19';
}

// ── Auth helper ──────────────────────────────────
function getToken() {
  return localStorage.getItem('access_token') || '';
}

async function refreshIfNeeded() {
  const token = getToken();
  if (!token) return null;
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/auth/me', {
      headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token }
    });
    if (res.status === 401) {
      const refresh = localStorage.getItem('refresh_token');
      if (!refresh) return null;
      const r = await fetch('/auth/refresh', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ refresh_token: refresh })
      });
      if (!r.ok) return null;
      const data = await r.json();
      localStorage.setItem('access_token', data.access_token);
      localStorage.setItem('refresh_token', data.refresh_token);
      return data.access_token;
    }
    return token;
  } catch { return token; }
}

// ── Risk badge ───────────────────────────────────
function riskClass(val) {
  if (!val) return 'risk-med';
  const s = String(val).toUpperCase();
  if (s.startsWith('H') || s.includes('HIGH')) return 'risk-high';
  if (s.startsWith('L') || s.includes('LOW')) return 'risk-low';
  return 'risk-med';
}

// ── Render task cards ────────────────────────────
function renderTaskCards() {
  const container = document.getElementById('task-cards');
  container.innerHTML = '';
  tasks.forEach((task, idx) => {
    const card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'task-card';
    card.id = 'task-card-' + idx;
    card.innerHTML = buildCardHeader(task, idx) + buildCardBody(task, idx);
    container.appendChild(card);
  });
}

function buildCardHeader(task, idx) {
  return `<div class="task-card-header" onclick="toggleCard(${idx})">
    <span class="task-num">${idx + 1}</span>
    <input class="task-name-input" type="text" value="${esc(task.task || '')}"
      onclick="event.stopPropagation()" oninput="tasks[${idx}].task=this.value">
    <span class="risk-badge ${riskClass(task.risk_pre)}">${esc(task.risk_pre || 'M')}</span>
    <span style="color:var(--text-muted)">\u2192</span>
    <span class="risk-badge ${riskClass(task.risk_post)}">${esc(task.risk_post || 'L')}</span>
    <span class="collapse-icon">\u25BC</span>
  </div>`;
}

function buildCardBody(task, idx) {
  let html = '<div class="task-card-body">';

  // Scope
  html += `<div class="field-group">
    <div class="field-group-label">Scope</div>
    <textarea oninput="tasks[${idx}].scope=this.value">${esc(task.scope || '')}</textarea>
  </div>`;

  // List fields
  html += buildListGroup('Hazards', 'hazards', task.hazards || [], idx, '');
  html += buildListGroup('Controls', 'controls', task.controls || [], idx, '');
  html += buildListGroup('PPE', 'ppe', task.ppe || [], idx, '');
  html += buildListGroup('\u26a0\ufe0f HOLD POINT', 'hold_points', task.hold_points || [], idx, '');
  html += buildListGroup('\ud83d\uded1 STOP WORK', 'stop_work', task.stop_work || [], idx, '');

  html += '</div>';
  return html;
}

function buildListGroup(label, field, items, taskIdx, prefix) {
  let html = `<div class="field-group">
    <div class="field-group-label">${label}</div>`;
  items.forEach((item, itemIdx) => {
    html += `<div class="list-item">
      <input type="text" value="${esc(item)}"
        oninput="tasks[${taskIdx}].${field}[${itemIdx}]=this.value">
      <button class="remove-btn" onclick="removeItem(${taskIdx},'${field}',${itemIdx})">&times;</button>
    </div>`;
  });
  html += `<button class="add-btn" onclick="addItem(${taskIdx},'${field}')">+ Add ${label.replace(/[^\w\s]/g,'').trim().toLowerCase()}</button>`;
  html += '</div>';
  return html;
}

function toggleCard(idx) {
  document.getElementById('task-card-' + idx).classList.toggle('collapsed');
}

function removeItem(taskIdx, field, itemIdx) {
  tasks[taskIdx][field].splice(itemIdx, 1);
  renderTaskCards();
}

function addItem(taskIdx, field) {
  if (!tasks[taskIdx][field]) tasks[taskIdx][field] = [];
  tasks[taskIdx][field].push('');
  renderTaskCards();
  // Focus the new empty input
  const card = document.getElementById('task-card-' + taskIdx);
  const inputs = card.querySelectorAll('.field-group input[type="text"]');
  if (inputs.length) inputs[inputs.length - 1].focus();
}

function esc(s) {
  return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
}

// ── Render sidebar ───────────────────────────────
function renderSidebar() {
  // HRCW
  const hrcwBox = document.getElementById('sidebar-hrcw');
  if (inference.hrcw) {
    hrcwBox.innerHTML = `<div class="sidebar-title">HRCW</div>
      <div class="hrcw-box">${esc(inference.hrcw_category || 'High Risk Construction Work identified')}</div>`;
  } else {
    hrcwBox.innerHTML = `<div class="sidebar-title">HRCW</div>
      <div class="hrcw-none">No HRCW identified</div>`;
  }

  // PPE
  renderCheckList('ppe-list', inference.ppe || []);

  // Certifications
  renderCheckList('certs-list', inference.certifications || []);

  // Permits
  renderCheckList('permits-list', inference.permits || []);

  // Legislation
  const legEl = document.getElementById('legislation-list');
  const notes = inference.regulatory_notes || [];
  legEl.innerHTML = notes.length
    ? notes.map(n => `<div class="sidebar-plain">${esc(n)}</div>`).join('')
    : '<div class="sidebar-plain" style="color:var(--text-muted)">None</div>';
}

function renderCheckList(containerId, items) {
  const el = document.getElementById(containerId);
  if (!items.length) {
    el.innerHTML = '<div class="sidebar-plain" style="color:var(--text-muted)">None</div>';
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = items.map((item, i) =>
    `<label class="sidebar-check">
      <input type="checkbox" checked data-list="${containerId}" data-index="${i}">
      ${esc(item)}
    </label>`
  ).join('');
}

// ── Confirm bar ──────────────────────────────────
function onConfirmCheck() {
  document.getElementById('confirm-btn').disabled = !document.getElementById('confirm-checkbox').checked;
  document.getElementById('confirm-error').textContent = '';
}

async function confirmAndDownload() {
  const errorEl = document.getElementById('confirm-error');
  errorEl.textContent = '';
  const btn = document.getElementById('confirm-btn');
  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.textContent = 'Generating\u2026';

  try {
    // Collect filtered inference
    const filteredInference = {...inference};
    filteredInference.ppe = getCheckedItems('ppe-list', inference.ppe || []);
    filteredInference.certifications = getCheckedItems('certs-list', inference.certifications || []);
    filteredInference.permits = getCheckedItems('permits-list', inference.permits || []);

    // Update meta
    const reviewer = localStorage.getItem('user_name') || meta.supervisor || meta.manager_name || 'Reviewer';
    meta.reviewed_by = reviewer;
    meta.reviewed_at = new Date().toISOString();

    const format = localStorage.getItem('swms_format') || 'pdf';
    const payload = {
      tasks: tasks,
      project_meta: meta,
      inference: filteredInference,
      jurisdiction: meta.jurisdiction || 'AU',
    };

    let renderUrl;
    if (format === 'both') renderUrl = '/render/both';
    else if (format === 'pdf') renderUrl = '/render/pdf';
    else renderUrl = '/render/docx';

    const token = await refreshIfNeeded();
    if (!token) {
      throw new Error('Not authenticated — please log in again.');
    }
    const res = await fetch(API + renderUrl, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token,
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    });

    if (!res.ok) {
      const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      throw new Error(err.detail || err.error || 'Render failed: ' + res.status);
    }

    btn.textContent = 'Preparing download\u2026';

    try {
      if (format === 'both') {
        const data = await res.json();
        console.log('Both response received:', Object.keys(data));
        if (!data.docx || !data.pdf) {
          throw new Error('Empty response from render endpoint');
        }
        pendingBlob = null;
        pendingFilename = data.docx_filename;
        downloadBase64(data.docx, data.docx_filename,
          'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document');
        setTimeout(() => downloadBase64(data.pdf, data.pdf_filename, 'application/pdf'), 600);
      } else {
        const blob = await res.blob();
        console.log('Blob received:', blob.size, blob.type);
        if (blob.size === 0) {
          throw new Error('Empty response from render endpoint');
        }
        const cd = res.headers.get('content-disposition') || '';
        const match = cd.match(/filename="?([^"]+)"?/);
        const filename = match ? match[1] : 'SWMS.' + (format === 'pdf' ? 'pdf' : 'docx');
        pendingBlob = blob;
        pendingFilename = filename;
      }
    } catch (dlErr) {
      console.error('Download error:', dlErr);
      throw new Error('Download failed: ' + dlErr.message);
    }

    // Clear localStorage
    localStorage.removeItem('swms_review_tasks');
    localStorage.removeItem('swms_review_inference');
    localStorage.removeItem('swms_review_meta');

    // Show disclaimer
    showDisclaimerModal();

  } catch (err) {
    errorEl.textContent = err.message;
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = 'Confirm & Download';
  }
}

function getCheckedItems(containerId, original) {
  const checks = document.querySelectorAll(`#${containerId} input[type="checkbox"]`);
  const result = [];
  checks.forEach((cb, i) => {
    if (cb.checked && original[i]) result.push(original[i]);
  });
  return result;
}

function downloadBase64(b64, filename, mime) {
  const bin = atob(b64);
  const arr = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) arr[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  const blob = new Blob([arr], {type: mime});
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url; a.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
}

// ── Disclaimer modal ─────────────────────────────
function showDisclaimerModal() {
  document.getElementById('disclaimer-checkbox').checked = false;
  document.getElementById('disclaimer-accept-btn').disabled = true;
  document.getElementById('disclaimer-overlay').classList.add('visible');
}

function onDisclaimerCheck() {
  document.getElementById('disclaimer-accept-btn').disabled =
    !document.getElementById('disclaimer-checkbox').checked;
}

function acceptAndDownload() {
  document.getElementById('disclaimer-overlay').classList.remove('visible');
  if (pendingBlob) {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(pendingBlob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url; a.download = pendingFilename;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    setTimeout(() => {
      a.click();
      setTimeout(() => {
        document.body.removeChild(a);
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      }, 1000);
    }, 100);
  }
  // Redirect back after download
  setTimeout(() => { window.location.href = '/swms'; }, 2500);
}

function declineDisclaimer() {
  document.getElementById('disclaimer-overlay').classList.remove('visible');
}
</script>

</body>
</html>
